          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/pottery",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/pottery.json",
          "text": "A Shovel for all your digging purposes.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.21.text",
          "label": "Pottery",
          "item_ref_count": 10,
          "recipe_ref_count": 14,
          "count": 22
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/buildinggadget",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/buildinggadget.json",
          "text": "Building Gadget",
          "key": "guide-page:name",
          "label": "Building Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 4,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/buildinggadget",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/buildinggadget.json",
          "text": "The building gadget makes building a large number of blocks all at once extremely easy! First, ensure the gadget has some energy. Second, Shift right-click a block in the world to specify the block to build with.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Building Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 4,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/buildinggadget",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/buildinggadget.json",
          "text": "You can choose your Building Mode by holding the settings key. Each mode is described in its appropriate entry in this book. The default mode is Build to Me. When looking at a block in the world, you'll see a ghostly image of the blocks that will be placed. If you're missing the required items or energy to place these blocks, they will have a red overlay. Right click to place the blocks as shown.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.text",
          "label": "Building Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 4,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/buildinggadget",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/buildinggadget.json",
          "text": "Build Preview",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.title",
          "label": "Building Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 4,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/buildinggadget",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/buildinggadget.json",
          "text": "Seeing a preview of what will be built",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.text",
          "label": "Building Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 4,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/buildinggadget",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/buildinggadget.json",
          "text": "When you right click, building will begin. Items will be taken out of your inventory to be placed into the world, and any blocks you don't have the necessary item for will be skipped. Don't forget you can undo if you make a mistake!",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.text",
          "label": "Building Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 4,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/copypaste",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/copypaste.json",
          "text": "Copy Paste Gadget",
          "key": "guide-page:name",
          "label": "Copy Paste Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 10
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/copypaste",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/copypaste.json",
          "text": "The copy paste gadget makes replicating structures far easier! To start, ensure you're in 'copy' mode in the settings menu. Right click one corner of a structure,and then shift-right click an opposing corner. If done correctly, a green outline will surround your structure.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Copy Paste Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 10
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/copypaste",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/copypaste.json",
          "text": "Copying a structure",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.text",
          "label": "Copy Paste Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 10
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/copypaste",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/copypaste.json",
          "text": "Switch the gadget to 'paste' mode, and you'll see a ghostly preview of the structure you've copied. The block you right clicked during copying is the starting point of the structure. To place the structure in world, simply right click! All items necessary for building will be removed from your inventory, and any blocks you don't have items for will be skipped.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.text",
          "label": "Copy Paste Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 10
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/copypaste",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/copypaste.json",
          "text": "Paste Preview",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.title",
          "label": "Copy Paste Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 10
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/copypaste",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/copypaste.json",
          "text": "Previewing a paste",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.text",
          "label": "Copy Paste Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 10
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/copypaste",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/copypaste.json",
          "text": "To see a list of the items you need, open the settings menu and choose the 'material list' option. This is a scrollable list showing what items you do or do not have, and can be sorted in multiple ways. Try it out!",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.4.text",
          "label": "Copy Paste Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 10
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/copypaste",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/copypaste.json",
          "text": "Material List",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.5.title",
          "label": "Copy Paste Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 10
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/copypaste",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/copypaste.json",
          "text": "Needed items",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.5.text",
          "label": "Copy Paste Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 10
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/copypaste",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/copypaste.json",
          "text": "Its possible to make fine tuned adjustments to the copy area by opening the Adjust Placement window in the settings menu. From here, you can adjust the position of the start block and end block. Click the plus and minus buttons to see the green box change in real time. You can also swap to 'absolute' coordinates mode and enter in-game world coordinates instead.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.6.text",
          "label": "Copy Paste Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 10
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/copypaste",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/copypaste.json",
          "text": "Adjusting Copy",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.7.title",
          "label": "Copy Paste Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 10
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/copypaste",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/copypaste.json",
          "text": "Changing the area you are copying",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.7.text",
          "label": "Copy Paste Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 10
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/copypaste",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/copypaste.json",
          "text": "Theres a similar option for paste mode. In this UI, you'll be adjusting the location of the starting block. Click the buttons and the render should update in-world in real time.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.8.text",
          "label": "Copy Paste Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 10
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/copypaste",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/copypaste.json",
          "text": "Adjusting Paste",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.9.title",
          "label": "Copy Paste Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 10
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/copypaste",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/copypaste.json",
          "text": "Changing the location of the paste",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.9.text",
          "label": "Copy Paste Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 10
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/cutpaste",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/cutpaste.json",
          "text": "Cut Paste Gadget",
          "key": "guide-page:name",
          "label": "Cut Paste Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/cutpaste",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/cutpaste.json",
          "text": "The cut paste gadget makes moving structures a breeze, and works just like the Copy Paste Gadget! To start, ensure you're in 'cut' mode in the settings menu. Right click one corner of a structure,and then shift-right click an opposing corner. If done correctly, a red outline will surround your structure.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Cut Paste Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/cutpaste",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/cutpaste.json",
          "text": "Cutting a structure",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.text",
          "label": "Cut Paste Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/cutpaste",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/cutpaste.json",
          "text": "The structure will not be removed until you click the 'cut' button in the settings menu. Switch the gadget to 'paste' mode, and you'll see a ghostly preview of the structure you've copied. To place the structure in world, simply right click! No items are necessary for this, as its using the blocks you removed earlier.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.text",
          "label": "Cut Paste Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/cutpaste",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/cutpaste.json",
          "text": "Paste Preview",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.title",
          "label": "Cut Paste Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/cutpaste",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/cutpaste.json",
          "text": "Previewing a paste",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.text",
          "label": "Cut Paste Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/cutpaste",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/cutpaste.json",
          "text": "You can only have one structure stored on the gadget at a time. Placing it in the world will erased the stored information on the gadget. Be careful when pasting the structure, you only get one chance, exchange mode is recommended! If you attempt to cut a new area while data already exists, you'll be prompted to confirm. If you do this, the first set of data will be lost forever!",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.4.text",
          "label": "Cut Paste Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/cutpaste",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/cutpaste.json",
          "text": "All block-entity data will be moved along with the structure, including the contents of chests and block configurations. Much like the Copy Paste Gadget, you can modify the cut or paste positions with the appropriate settings menu option. See that chapter for details!",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.5.text",
          "label": "Cut Paste Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/destruction",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/destruction.json",
          "text": "Destruction Gadget",
          "key": "guide-page:name",
          "label": "Destruction Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "count": 3
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/destruction",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/destruction.json",
          "text": "The destruction gadget makes clearing a large area very easy. Any blocks cleared will be voided!. First, open the settings menu and adjust the area. This area is with respect to the block (and block face) you are looking at. If you look at the top face of a block, 'up' will be relative to the player's screen.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Destruction Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "count": 3
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/destruction",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/destruction.json",
          "text": "Destruction Gadget",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.title",
          "label": "Destruction Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "count": 3
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/destruction",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/destruction.json",
          "text": "Destroying an area, click the arrow to see diffent pictures!",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.text",
          "label": "Destruction Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "count": 3
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/destruction",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/destruction.json",
          "text": "Once you're satisfied with the settings, right click on a block to void all the highlighted blocks. They will be removed from the world. You may undo this action if you make a mistake. It will not remove items from your inventory to undo, as the gadget is just returning the voided blocks to the world.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.text",
          "label": "Destruction Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "count": 3
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/exchanginggadget",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/exchanginggadget.json",
          "text": "Exchanging Gadget",
          "key": "guide-page:name",
          "label": "Exchanging Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 4,
          "count": 5
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/exchanginggadget",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/exchanginggadget.json",
          "text": "The exchanging gadget can swap one set of blocks for another! It cannot, however, replace air blocks. Use a Building Gadget for that! First, ensure the gadget has some energy. Second, Shift right-click a block in the world to specify the block to exchange to.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Exchanging Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 4,
          "count": 5
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/exchanginggadget",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/exchanginggadget.json",
          "text": "You can choose your Exchanging Mode by holding the settings key. Each mode is described in its appropriate entry in this book. The default mode is Surface. When looking at a block in the world, you'll see a ghostly image of the blocks that will be placed over top of existing blocks. If you're missing the required items or energy to place these blocks, they will have a red overlay. Right click to exchange the blocks as shown.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.text",
          "label": "Exchanging Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 4,
          "count": 5
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/exchanginggadget",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/exchanginggadget.json",
          "text": "Exchange Preview",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.title",
          "label": "Exchanging Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 4,
          "count": 5
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/exchanginggadget",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/exchanginggadget.json",
          "text": "Seeing a preview of what will be exchanged",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.text",
          "label": "Exchanging Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 4,
          "count": 5
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/exchanginggadget",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/exchanginggadget.json",
          "text": "When you right click, exchanging will begin. Items will be taken out of your inventory to be placed into the world, and any blocks you don't have the necessary item for will be skipped. Items for the removed blocks will be returned to you. Note: You can enchant this tool with silk touch. Without it grass will return dirt, etc.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.text",
          "label": "Exchanging Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 4,
          "count": 5
        }
      ],

        {

        {

          "text": "Just like trains, Postboxes maintain their behaviour in unloaded chunks",

          "source": "file:minecraft/lang-overlays",

        {

          "source": "file:minecraft/lang-overlays",

          "source": "file:minecraft/lang-overlays",

    {
      "id": "pack:tfg/getting_started_boats",
      "label": "Getting Started: Boats",
      "origin": "pack_generated",
      "confidence": 0.7,
      "support": 21,
      "evidence": [
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/firmaciv/watercraft",
          "confidence": 0.7
        }
      ],
      "semantic_evidence": [
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/firmaciv/watercraft",
          "source": "jar:FirmaCivilization-1.20.1-1.0.9.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/firmaciv/watercraft.json",
          "text": "Getting Started: Boats",
          "key": "guide-page:name",
          "label": "Getting Started: Boats",
          "item_ref_count": 14,
          "count": 21
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/firmaciv/watercraft",
          "source": "jar:FirmaCivilization-1.20.1-1.0.9.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/firmaciv/watercraft.json",
          "text": "Creating and using a boat may be slightly more involved than you're used to, but there's nothing quite like building the perfect craft and setting off on the high seas. Peruse this manual and learn the ins and outs of carving, crafting, constructing, rowing, paddling, piloting, and customizing to your delight.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Getting Started: Boats",
          "item_ref_count": 14,
          "count": 21
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/firmaciv/watercraft",
          "source": "jar:FirmaCivilization-1.20.1-1.0.9.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/firmaciv/watercraft.json",
          "text": "Travelling the world, or just your locality, is often best done by navigable waterways.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.text",
          "label": "Getting Started: Boats",
          "item_ref_count": 14,
          "count": 21
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/firmaciv/watercraft",
          "source": "jar:FirmaCivilization-1.20.1-1.0.9.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/firmaciv/watercraft.json",
          "text": "You can add blocks to boats! As if you're placing a block on land, press while holding a chest, an anvil, or a workbench, to add it to the boat. Press to access them. If you can't place a block in a particular place, that's probably because it's the pilot seat! You can't pilot the boat if there's a block in the way! You can remove the blocks by breaking them, like normal.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.text",
          "label": "Getting Started: Boats",
          "item_ref_count": 14,
          "count": 21
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/firmaciv/watercraft",
          "source": "jar:FirmaCivilization-1.20.1-1.0.9.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/firmaciv/watercraft.json",
          "text": "Boats as Items",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.4.title",
          "label": "Getting Started: Boats",
          "item_ref_count": 14,
          "count": 21
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/firmaciv/watercraft",
          "source": "jar:FirmaCivilization-1.20.1-1.0.9.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/firmaciv/watercraft.json",
          "text": "They don't exist! With the exception of the kayak, if you break a boat, it will only drop some of its ingredients! This means you'll need a kayak if you want to carry a boat over land, and it also means you might want to build a dock for your larger vessels.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.4.text",
          "label": "Getting Started: Boats",
          "item_ref_count": 14,
          "count": 21
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/firmaciv/watercraft",
          "source": "jar:FirmaCivilization-1.20.1-1.0.9.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/firmaciv/watercraft.json",
          "text": "Transporting Animals",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.6.title",
          "label": "Getting Started: Boats",
          "item_ref_count": 14,
          "count": 21
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/firmaciv/watercraft",
          "source": "jar:FirmaCivilization-1.20.1-1.0.9.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/firmaciv/watercraft.json",
          "text": "Like always, you can move animals around using boats! However, be aware that larger animals, like horses, will only fit in boats that are big enough. If an animal is riding a boat and is in your way, you only have to crouch and press to eject it from the boat. Animals will get restless after 2 days in the boat and will leave the boat on their own in 3 days.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.6.text",
          "label": "Getting Started: Boats",
          "item_ref_count": 14,
          "count": 21
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/firmaciv/watercraft",
          "source": "jar:FirmaCivilization-1.20.1-1.0.9.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/firmaciv/watercraft.json",
          "text": "Each boat has a specific seat that you need to be riding in order to control the boat.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.7.text",
          "label": "Getting Started: Boats",
          "item_ref_count": 14,
          "count": 21
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/firmaciv/watercraft",
          "source": "jar:FirmaCivilization-1.20.1-1.0.9.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/firmaciv/watercraft.json",
          "text": "Paddles and Oars",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.8.title",
          "label": "Getting Started: Boats",
          "item_ref_count": 14,
          "count": 21
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/firmaciv/watercraft",
          "source": "jar:FirmaCivilization-1.20.1-1.0.9.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/firmaciv/watercraft.json",
          "text": "If you feel like your boat is moving slowly, double check that you're holding the appropriate paddle item, or, if your boat requires oars, that the oars have been equipped to the oarlocks. In some boats, multiple players are able to paddle or row to make the boat go faster.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.8.text",
          "label": "Getting Started: Boats",
          "item_ref_count": 14,
          "count": 21
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/firmaciv/watercraft",
          "source": "jar:FirmaCivilization-1.20.1-1.0.9.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/firmaciv/watercraft.json",
          "text": "Cleats and Leads",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.10.title",
          "label": "Getting Started: Boats",
          "item_ref_count": 14,
          "count": 21
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/firmaciv/watercraft",
          "source": "jar:FirmaCivilization-1.20.1-1.0.9.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/firmaciv/watercraft.json",
          "text": "Most boats have at least one cleat on them. You can attach a lead to the cleat and use it to pull the boat, and pull small boats up onto the shoreline and up slabs. Leads can also be used to attach boats to fences, and attaching two cleats each to a fence will allow you to easily dock larger boats. You'll find yourself wanting to use leads extensively to stop your boats from drifting away!",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.10.text",
          "label": "Getting Started: Boats",
          "item_ref_count": 14,
          "count": 21
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/firmaciv/watercraft",
          "source": "jar:FirmaCivilization-1.20.1-1.0.9.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/firmaciv/watercraft.json",
          "text": "Anchors and Windlasses",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.12.title",
          "label": "Getting Started: Boats",
          "item_ref_count": 14,
          "count": 21
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/firmaciv/watercraft",
          "source": "jar:FirmaCivilization-1.20.1-1.0.9.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/firmaciv/watercraft.json",
          "text": "Large boats have anchors, which can be used to completely stop the boat, provided the anchor is seated in the seafloor and no sails are deployed. The boat will still turn in the wind while it's at anchor. Look for the anchor icon located at the windlass (anchor winch) and press to lower and raise the anchor.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.12.text",
          "label": "Getting Started: Boats",
          "item_ref_count": 14,
          "count": 21
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/firmaciv/watercraft",
          "source": "jar:FirmaCivilization-1.20.1-1.0.9.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/firmaciv/watercraft.json",
          "text": "The anchor and windlass on a sloop.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.13.text",
          "label": "Getting Started: Boats",
          "item_ref_count": 14,
          "count": 21
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/firmaciv/watercraft",
          "source": "jar:FirmaCivilization-1.20.1-1.0.9.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/firmaciv/watercraft.json",
          "text": "Sailing vessels, of course, have sails. You'll need to deploy them in order to properly control your boat. Look for the triangular sail icon and press to deploy each sail. Since most boats have multiple sails, you can use different combinations of sail deployment to control your speed.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.14.text",
          "label": "Getting Started: Boats",
          "item_ref_count": 14,
          "count": 21
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/firmaciv/watercraft",
          "source": "jar:FirmaCivilization-1.20.1-1.0.9.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/firmaciv/watercraft.json",
          "text": "Both the jibsail (left) and mainsail (right) have been deployed on this sloop.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.15.text",
          "label": "Getting Started: Boats",
          "item_ref_count": 14,
          "count": 21
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/firmaciv/watercraft",
          "source": "jar:FirmaCivilization-1.20.1-1.0.9.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/firmaciv/watercraft.json",
          "text": "Unlike ships that you may be used to, sailing is a skill with Firma: Civilization. When you're piloting a sailing vessel, a new UI element will appear showing the wind speed and direction, as well as the boat's speed. The color of the wind arrow will indicate the difficulty of sailing in that direction. Use your forward and backward movement keys to set the angle of the sails, and experiment to find the best one for the wind angle.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.16.text",
          "label": "Getting Started: Boats",
          "item_ref_count": 14,
          "count": 21
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/firmaciv/watercraft",
          "source": "jar:FirmaCivilization-1.20.1-1.0.9.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/firmaciv/watercraft.json",
          "text": "The sailing UI will help you control your vessel.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.17.text",
          "label": "Getting Started: Boats",
          "item_ref_count": 14,
          "count": 21
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/firmaciv/watercraft",
          "source": "jar:FirmaCivilization-1.20.1-1.0.9.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/firmaciv/watercraft.json",
          "text": "Painting and Dyeing",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.18.title",
          "label": "Getting Started: Boats",
          "item_ref_count": 14,
          "count": 21
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/firmaciv/watercraft",
          "source": "jar:FirmaCivilization-1.20.1-1.0.9.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/firmaciv/watercraft.json",
          "text": "Any boat constructed using Shipwright's Scaffolding can be painted, and any sail can be individually dyed. Simply hold a dye item in your hand and press when you see the paint brush icon to either paint a hull or dye a sail. Larger boats have unique patterns for each paint color.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.18.text",
          "label": "Getting Started: Boats",
          "item_ref_count": 14,
          "count": 21
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/firmaciv/watercraft",
          "source": "jar:FirmaCivilization-1.20.1-1.0.9.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/firmaciv/watercraft.json",
          "text": "A sloop has been painted yellow and its sails have been dyed.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.19.text",
          "label": "Getting Started: Boats",
          "item_ref_count": 14,
          "count": 21
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/firmaciv/watercraft",
          "source": "jar:FirmaCivilization-1.20.1-1.0.9.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/firmaciv/watercraft.json",
          "text": "Damage and Repair",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.20.title",
          "label": "Getting Started: Boats",
          "item_ref_count": 14,
          "count": 21
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/firmaciv/watercraft",
          "source": "jar:FirmaCivilization-1.20.1-1.0.9.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/firmaciv/watercraft.json",
          "text": "A damaged boat will start to show cracks. For any boat built using Shipwright's Scaffolding, use the appropriate type of planks to repair it. For canoes, use lumber. At 90% damage, a boat will appear to take on water, and at 100% damage, it will sink! This does not apply to Kayaks.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.20.text",
          "label": "Getting Started: Boats",
          "item_ref_count": 14,
          "count": 21
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/buildinggadget",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/buildinggadget.json",
          "text": "Building Gadget",
          "key": "guide-page:name",
          "label": "Building Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 4,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/buildinggadget",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/buildinggadget.json",
          "text": "The building gadget makes building a large number of blocks all at once extremely easy! First, ensure the gadget has some energy. Second, Shift right-click a block in the world to specify the block to build with.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Building Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 4,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/buildinggadget",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/buildinggadget.json",
          "text": "You can choose your Building Mode by holding the settings key. Each mode is described in its appropriate entry in this book. The default mode is Build to Me. When looking at a block in the world, you'll see a ghostly image of the blocks that will be placed. If you're missing the required items or energy to place these blocks, they will have a red overlay. Right click to place the blocks as shown.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.text",
          "label": "Building Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 4,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/buildinggadget",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/buildinggadget.json",

          "label": "Building Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 4,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/buildinggadget",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/buildinggadget.json",
          "text": "Seeing a preview of what will be built",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.text",
          "label": "Building Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 4,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/buildinggadget",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/buildinggadget.json",
          "text": "When you right click, building will begin. Items will be taken out of your inventory to be placed into the world, and any blocks you don't have the necessary item for will be skipped. Don't forget you can undo if you make a mistake!",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.text",
          "label": "Building Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 4,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/copypaste",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/copypaste.json",
          "text": "Copy Paste Gadget",

          "label": "Copy Paste Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 10
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/copypaste",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/copypaste.json",
          "text": "The copy paste gadget makes replicating structures far easier! To start, ensure you're in 'copy' mode in the settings menu. Right click one corner of a structure,and then shift-right click an opposing corner. If done correctly, a green outline will surround your structure.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Copy Paste Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 10
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/copypaste",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/copypaste.json",
          "text": "Copying a structure",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.text",
          "label": "Copy Paste Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 10
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",

          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/copypaste.json",
          "text": "Switch the gadget to 'paste' mode, and you'll see a ghostly preview of the structure you've copied. The block you right clicked during copying is the starting point of the structure. To place the structure in world, simply right click! All items necessary for building will be removed from your inventory, and any blocks you don't have items for will be skipped.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.text",
          "label": "Copy Paste Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 10
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/copypaste",

          "text": "Paste Preview",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.title",
          "label": "Copy Paste Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 10
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/copypaste",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/copypaste.json",
          "text": "Previewing a paste",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.text",
          "label": "Copy Paste Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 10
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/copypaste",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/copypaste.json",
          "text": "To see a list of the items you need, open the settings menu and choose the 'material list' option. This is a scrollable list showing what items you do or do not have, and can be sorted in multiple ways. Try it out!",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.4.text",
          "label": "Copy Paste Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 10
        },
        {

        {

        {

          "text": "Its possible to make fine tuned adjustments to the copy area by opening the Adjust Placement window in the settings menu.           "key": "guide-page:pages.6.text",
          "label": "Copy Paste Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 10
        },
        {

        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/copypaste",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/copypaste.json",
          "text": "Changing the area you are copying",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.7.text",
          "label": "Copy Paste Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 10
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",

          "text": "Theres a similar option for paste mode. In this UI, you'll be adjusting the location of the starting block. Click the buttons and the render should update in-world in real time.",

        {
          "kind": "guide_page",

          "text": "The cut paste gadget makes moving structures a breeze, and works just like the Copy Paste Gadget! To start, ensure you're in 'cut' mode in the settings menu. Right click one corner of a structure,and then shift-right click an opposing corner. If done correctly, a red outline will surround your structure.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Cut Paste Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/cutpaste",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/cutpaste.json",
          "text": "Cutting a structure",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.text",
          "label": "Cut Paste Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/cutpaste",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/cutpaste.json",
          "text": "The structure will not be removed until you click the 'cut' button in the settings menu. Switch the gadget to 'paste' mode, and you'll see a ghostly preview of the structure you've copied. To place the structure in world, simply right click! No items are necessary for this, as its using the blocks you removed earlier.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.text",
          "label": "Cut Paste Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/cutpaste",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/cutpaste.json",
          "text": "Paste Preview",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.title",
          "label": "Cut Paste Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/cutpaste",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/cutpaste.json",
          "text": "Previewing a paste",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.text",
          "label": "Cut Paste Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/cutpaste",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/cutpaste.json",
          "text": "You can only have one structure stored on the gadget at a time. Placing it in the world will erased the stored information on the gadget. Be careful when pasting the structure, you only get one chance, exchange mode is recommended! If you attempt to cut a new area while data already exists, you'll be prompted to confirm. If you do this, the first set of data will be lost forever!",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.4.text",
          "label": "Cut Paste Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/cutpaste",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/cutpaste.json",
          "text": "All block-entity data will be moved along with the structure, including the contents of chests and block configurations. Much like the Copy Paste Gadget, you can modify the cut or paste positions with the appropriate settings menu option. See that chapter for details!",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.5.text",
          "label": "Cut Paste Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/destruction",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/destruction.json",
          "text": "Destruction Gadget",
          "key": "guide-page:name",
          "label": "Destruction Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "count": 3
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/destruction",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/destruction.json",
          "text": "The destruction gadget makes clearing a large area very easy. Any blocks cleared will be voided!. First, open the settings menu and adjust the area. This area is with respect to the block (and block face) you are looking at. If you look at the top face of a block, 'up' will be relative to the player's screen.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Destruction Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "count": 3
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/destruction",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/destruction.json",
          "text": "Destruction Gadget",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.title",
          "label": "Destruction Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "count": 3
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/destruction",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/destruction.json",
          "text": "Destroying an area, click the arrow to see diffent pictures!",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.text",
          "label": "Destruction Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "count": 3
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/destruction",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/destruction.json",
          "text": "Once you're satisfied with the settings, right click on a block to void all the highlighted blocks. They will be removed from the world. You may undo this action if you make a mistake. It will not remove items from your inventory to undo, as the gadget is just returning the voided blocks to the world.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.text",
          "label": "Destruction Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "count": 3
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/exchanginggadget",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/exchanginggadget.json",
          "text": "Exchanging Gadget",
          "key": "guide-page:name",
          "label": "Exchanging Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 4,
          "count": 5
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/exchanginggadget",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/exchanginggadget.json",
          "text": "The exchanging gadget can swap one set of blocks for another! It cannot, however, replace air blocks. Use a Building Gadget for that! First, ensure the gadget has some energy. Second, Shift right-click a block in the world to specify the block to exchange to.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Exchanging Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 4,
          "count": 5
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/exchanginggadget",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/exchanginggadget.json",
          "text": "You can choose your Exchanging Mode by holding the settings key. Each mode is described in its appropriate entry in this book. The default mode is Surface. When looking at a block in the world, you'll see a ghostly image of the blocks that will be placed over top of existing blocks. If you're missing the required items or energy to place these blocks, they will have a red overlay. Right click to exchange the blocks as shown.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.text",
          "label": "Exchanging Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 4,
          "count": 5
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/exchanginggadget",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/exchanginggadget.json",
          "text": "Exchange Preview",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.title",
          "label": "Exchanging Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 4,
          "count": 5
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",

          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/exchanginggadget.json",
          "text": "Seeing a preview of what will be exchanged",

      "semantic_evidence": [
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
