      "semantic_evidence": [
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/prospecting",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/prospecting.json",
          "text": "You remembered where you picked up those small metal nuggets, right? Finding additional ores may require extensive exploration and mining. You should become very familiar with ores and minerals. If you need a specific resource, you must find the rock type it spawns in either under your feet or across the world.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Prospecting",
          "item_ref_count": 12,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 12
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/prospecting",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/prospecting.json",
          "text": "When picking up small nuggets becomes unsatisfying, it is time to start prospecting to find ore veins: - Small nuggets, surface buds, and surface rocks occur when an ore vein is located below. If you find the center of a group of nuggets, it's likely that the vein is beneath you. - Exposed ore can occur in cliffs and water bodies, which may be seen from farther away.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.text",
          "label": "Prospecting",
          "item_ref_count": 12,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 12
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/prospecting",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/prospecting.json",
          "text": "Prospector's Pick",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.title",
          "label": "Prospecting",
          "item_ref_count": 12,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 12
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/prospecting",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/prospecting.json",
          "text": "If you're looking for ore veins, and you can't find the vein by guessing, it's time to pull out the prospector's pick. If you are familiar with base prospector's picks in TFC you may be suprised to learn that they function very differently in TerraFirmaGreg",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.text",
          "label": "Prospecting",
          "item_ref_count": 12,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 12
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/prospecting",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/prospecting.json",
          "text": "Right-clicking a prospector's pick in TFG will cast a search ray in your views direction. The rays width and depth is dependent on the tier of the pick noted in its tooltips. Depending on the tier of the prospector's pick can also give you a count of the ore quantities and even display an X-Ray indicator of the ore vein.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.text",
          "label": "Prospecting",
          "item_ref_count": 12,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 12
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/prospecting",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/prospecting.json",
          "text": "The prospector's pick can display the following information on use: - In chat the pick can display the total quantity of the ore vein, the contents of the ore vein, and the quantity per ore-type of the ore vein. - colored steel prospector's picks can also display either a large X-Ray particle in the center of an ore vein, or paricle's per ore block",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.4.text",
          "label": "Prospecting",
          "item_ref_count": 12,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 12
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/prospecting",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/prospecting.json",
          "text": "Prospector View",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.5.title",
          "label": "Prospecting",
          "item_ref_count": 12,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 12
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/prospecting",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/prospecting.json",
          "text": "Using a prospector's pick looks like this. The large \"!\" mark particle is from per-vein style x-ray. And the smaller ones are from per-block style x-ray.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.5.text",
          "label": "Prospecting",
          "item_ref_count": 12,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 12
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/prospecting",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/prospecting.json",
          "text": "To make a prospector's pick, you can knapp an unfired mold out of clay as shown above.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.6.text",
          "label": "Prospecting",
          "item_ref_count": 12,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 12
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/prospecting",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/prospecting.json",
          "text": "Once the mold has been knapped, it needs to be fired to create a propick head mold. To create the tool head, cast liquid metal into the mold.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.7.text",
          "label": "Prospecting",
          "item_ref_count": 12,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 12
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/prospecting",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/prospecting.json",
          "text": "A prospector's pick head can also be smithed out of an ingot of any tool metal on an Anvil. The prospector's pick is then created by crafting the tool head with a stick.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.8.text",
          "label": "Prospecting",
          "item_ref_count": 12,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 12
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/prospecting",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/prospecting.json",
          "text": "The majority of the stats and charecteristics of the prospector's picks can be adjustable in your server config file.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.9.text",
          "label": "Prospecting",
          "item_ref_count": 12,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 12
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/prospecting",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/prospecting.json",
          "text": "Digital Ore Prospector's",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.10.title",
          "label": "Prospecting",
          "item_ref_count": 12,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 12
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/prospecting",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/prospecting.json",
          "text": "Technology is now! And with it comes new ways to find your ore veins! Ore Prospector's are GregTech's solution to finding ore veins and fluid deposits in your world. The size and capabilities of the ore prospectors are determined by the voltage tier.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.10.text",
          "label": "Prospecting",
          "item_ref_count": 12,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 12
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/prospecting",

          "text": "Ore Prospector",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.11.title",
          "label": "Prospecting",

          "recipe_ref_count": 3,

        },
        {

          "text": "An example of what you may see using the LuV ore prospector.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.11.text",
          "label": "Prospecting",
          "item_ref_count": 12,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 12
        },

        {
          "kind": "runtime_item",
          "id": "gtceu:prospector.lv",
          "source": "runtime-items",
          "text": "-Ore Prospection Mode",
          "key": "runtime-tooltip",
          "label": "Ore Prospector (LV)",
          "item_ref_count": 1
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/buildinggadget",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/buildinggadget.json",
          "text": "Building Gadget",
          "key": "guide-page:name",
          "label": "Building Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 4,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/buildinggadget",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/buildinggadget.json",
          "text": "The building gadget makes building a large number of blocks all at once extremely easy! First, ensure the gadget has some energy. Second, Shift right-click a block in the world to specify the block to build with.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Building Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 4,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/buildinggadget",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/buildinggadget.json",
          "text": "You can choose your Building Mode by holding the settings key. Each mode is described in its appropriate entry in this book. The default mode is Build to Me. When looking at a block in the world, you'll see a ghostly image of the blocks that will be placed. If you're missing the required items or energy to place these blocks, they will have a red overlay. Right click to place the blocks as shown.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.text",
          "label": "Building Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 4,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/buildinggadget",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/buildinggadget.json",
          "text": "Build Preview",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.title",
          "label": "Building Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 4,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/buildinggadget",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/buildinggadget.json",
          "text": "Seeing a preview of what will be built",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.text",
          "label": "Building Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 4,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/buildinggadget",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/buildinggadget.json",
          "text": "When you right click, building will begin. Items will be taken out of your inventory to be placed into the world, and any blocks you don't have the necessary item for will be skipped. Don't forget you can undo if you make a mistake!",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.text",
          "label": "Building Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 4,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/copypaste",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/copypaste.json",
          "text": "Copy Paste Gadget",
          "key": "guide-page:name",
          "label": "Copy Paste Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 10
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/copypaste",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/copypaste.json",
          "text": "The copy paste gadget makes replicating structures far easier! To start, ensure you're in 'copy' mode in the settings menu. Right click one corner of a structure,and then shift-right click an opposing corner. If done correctly, a green outline will surround your structure.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Copy Paste Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 10
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/copypaste",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/copypaste.json",
          "text": "Copying a structure",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.text",
          "label": "Copy Paste Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 10
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/copypaste",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/copypaste.json",
          "text": "Switch the gadget to 'paste' mode, and you'll see a ghostly preview of the structure you've copied. The block you right clicked during copying is the starting point of the structure. To place the structure in world, simply right click! All items necessary for building will be removed from your inventory, and any blocks you don't have items for will be skipped.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.text",
          "label": "Copy Paste Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 10
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/copypaste",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/copypaste.json",
          "text": "Paste Preview",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.title",
          "label": "Copy Paste Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 10
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/copypaste",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/copypaste.json",
          "text": "Previewing a paste",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.text",
          "label": "Copy Paste Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 10
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/copypaste",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/copypaste.json",
          "text": "To see a list of the items you need, open the settings menu and choose the 'material list' option. This is a scrollable list showing what items you do or do not have, and can be sorted in multiple ways. Try it out!",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.4.text",
          "label": "Copy Paste Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 10
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/copypaste",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/copypaste.json",
          "text": "Material List",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.5.title",
          "label": "Copy Paste Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 10
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/copypaste",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/copypaste.json",
          "text": "Needed items",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.5.text",
          "label": "Copy Paste Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 10
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/copypaste",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/copypaste.json",
          "text": "Its possible to make fine tuned adjustments to the copy area by opening the Adjust Placement window in the settings menu. From here, you can adjust the position of the start block and end block. Click the plus and minus buttons to see the green box change in real time. You can also swap to 'absolute' coordinates mode and enter in-game world coordinates instead.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.6.text",
          "label": "Copy Paste Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 10
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/copypaste",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/copypaste.json",
          "text": "Adjusting Copy",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.7.title",
          "label": "Copy Paste Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 10
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/copypaste",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/copypaste.json",
          "text": "Changing the area you are copying",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.7.text",
          "label": "Copy Paste Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 10
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/copypaste",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/copypaste.json",
          "text": "Theres a similar option for paste mode. In this UI, you'll be adjusting the location of the starting block. Click the buttons and the render should update in-world in real time.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.8.text",
          "label": "Copy Paste Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 10
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/copypaste",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/copypaste.json",
          "text": "Adjusting Paste",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.9.title",
          "label": "Copy Paste Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 10
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/copypaste",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/copypaste.json",
          "text": "Changing the location of the paste",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.9.text",
          "label": "Copy Paste Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "count": 10
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/cutpaste",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/cutpaste.json",
          "text": "Cut Paste Gadget",
          "key": "guide-page:name",
          "label": "Cut Paste Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/cutpaste",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/cutpaste.json",
          "text": "The cut paste gadget makes moving structures a breeze, and works just like the Copy Paste Gadget! To start, ensure you're in 'cut' mode in the settings menu. Right click one corner of a structure,and then shift-right click an opposing corner. If done correctly, a red outline will surround your structure.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Cut Paste Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/cutpaste",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/cutpaste.json",
          "text": "Cutting a structure",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.text",
          "label": "Cut Paste Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/cutpaste",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/cutpaste.json",
          "text": "The structure will not be removed until you click the 'cut' button in the settings menu. Switch the gadget to 'paste' mode, and you'll see a ghostly preview of the structure you've copied. To place the structure in world, simply right click! No items are necessary for this, as its using the blocks you removed earlier.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.text",
          "label": "Cut Paste Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/cutpaste",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/cutpaste.json",
          "text": "Paste Preview",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.title",
          "label": "Cut Paste Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/cutpaste",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/cutpaste.json",
          "text": "Previewing a paste",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.text",
          "label": "Cut Paste Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/cutpaste",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/cutpaste.json",
          "text": "You can only have one structure stored on the gadget at a time. Placing it in the world will erased the stored information on the gadget. Be careful when pasting the structure, you only get one chance, exchange mode is recommended! If you attempt to cut a new area while data already exists, you'll be prompted to confirm. If you do this, the first set of data will be lost forever!",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.4.text",
          "label": "Cut Paste Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/cutpaste",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/cutpaste.json",
          "text": "All block-entity data will be moved along with the structure, including the contents of chests and block configurations. Much like the Copy Paste Gadget, you can modify the cut or paste positions with the appropriate settings menu option. See that chapter for details!",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.5.text",
          "label": "Cut Paste Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/destruction",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/destruction.json",
          "text": "Destruction Gadget",
          "key": "guide-page:name",
          "label": "Destruction Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "count": 3
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/destruction",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/destruction.json",
          "text": "The destruction gadget makes clearing a large area very easy. Any blocks cleared will be voided!. First, open the settings menu and adjust the area. This area is with respect to the block (and block face) you are looking at. If you look at the top face of a block, 'up' will be relative to the player's screen.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Destruction Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "count": 3
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/destruction",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/destruction.json",
          "text": "Destruction Gadget",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.title",
          "label": "Destruction Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "count": 3
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/destruction",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/destruction.json",
          "text": "Destroying an area, click the arrow to see diffent pictures!",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.text",
          "label": "Destruction Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "count": 3
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/destruction",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/destruction.json",
          "text": "Once you're satisfied with the settings, right click on a block to void all the highlighted blocks. They will be removed from the world. You may undo this action if you make a mistake. It will not remove items from your inventory to undo, as the gadget is just returning the voided blocks to the world.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.text",
          "label": "Destruction Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "count": 3
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/exchanginggadget",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/exchanginggadget.json",
          "text": "Exchanging Gadget",
          "key": "guide-page:name",
          "label": "Exchanging Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 4,
          "count": 5
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/exchanginggadget",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/exchanginggadget.json",
          "text": "The exchanging gadget can swap one set of blocks for another! It cannot, however, replace air blocks. Use a Building Gadget for that! First, ensure the gadget has some energy. Second, Shift right-click a block in the world to specify the block to exchange to.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Exchanging Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 4,
          "count": 5
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/exchanginggadget",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/exchanginggadget.json",
          "text": "You can choose your Exchanging Mode by holding the settings key. Each mode is described in its appropriate entry in this book. The default mode is Surface. When looking at a block in the world, you'll see a ghostly image of the blocks that will be placed over top of existing blocks. If you're missing the required items or energy to place these blocks, they will have a red overlay. Right click to exchange the blocks as shown.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.text",
          "label": "Exchanging Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 4,
          "count": 5
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/exchanginggadget",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/exchanginggadget.json",
          "text": "Exchange Preview",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.title",
          "label": "Exchanging Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 4,
          "count": 5
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/exchanginggadget",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/exchanginggadget.json",
          "text": "Seeing a preview of what will be exchanged",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.text",
          "label": "Exchanging Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 4,
          "count": 5
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/exchanginggadget",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/exchanginggadget.json",
          "text": "When you right click, exchanging will begin. Items will be taken out of your inventory to be placed into the world, and any blocks you don't have the necessary item for will be skipped. Items for the removed blocks will be returned to you. Note: You can enchant this tool with silk touch. Without it grass will return dirt, etc.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.text",
          "label": "Exchanging Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 4,
          "count": 5
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/exchanginggadget",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/exchanginggadget.json",
          "text": "You can undo with the exchanging gadget, but you must have the necessary items. For example, if you exchanged grass into stone bricks, you must have grass in your inventory to undo this exchange. Don't forget to apply silk touch if desired!",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.4.text",
          "label": "Exchanging Gadget",
          "item_ref_count": 4,
          "count": 5
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "buildinggadgets2:buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/mech_anchor",
          "source": "jar:buildinggadgets2-1.0.8.jar!assets/buildinggadgets2/patchouli_books/buildinggadgets2book/en_us/entries/mech_anchor.json",
          "text": "Anchor allows you to 'lock in' the block you're looking at, so you can freely move or look around. There is a hotkey for this, or you can access it from the settings menu. You can right click at any time to build the structure when it is anchored. Note: Changing range may not take effect until you remove and replace the anchor.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",

          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/firmaciv/rowboat.json",
          "text": "So, you've reached the Bronze Age and you want an upgrade? Well, the rowboat is for you. It has three seats, so it can hold 3 players, or up to 2 animals, including horses! As always, you can also replace seats (except for the pilot seat) with containers of your choosing. You can also place two additional containers at the stern.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Rowboats",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 10
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/firmaciv/rowboat",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/firmaciv/rowboat.json",
          "text": "In the rowboat, unlike other boats, you'll be sitting backwards when you're piloting the boat. It gives you a mechanical advantage so you can really put your back into those oars! You can, of course, pilot it in first-person, but if you may find it more intuitive in third-person facing yourself.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.text",
          "label": "Rowboats",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 10
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/firmaciv/rowboat",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/firmaciv/rowboat.json",
          "text": "To build a rowboat, you'll need to start with Shipwright's Scaffolding. These allow you to keep the boat materials in place while you build. Then, you'll place them facing inward in a 2 by 3 area.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.text",
          "label": "Rowboats",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 10
        },
        {

          "text": "Rowboat Scaffolding",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.4.name",
          "label": "Rowboats",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 10
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/firmaciv/rowboat",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/firmaciv/rowboat.json",

          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 10
        },
        {

          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/firmaciv/rowboat.json",
          "text": "Next, you'll need to add planks of suitably a strong hardwood: Acacia Ash Aspen Birch Blackwood Chestnut Hickory Maple Oak Rosewood Sycamore",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.5.text",
          "label": "Rowboats",

          "text": "Four planks have been added to each scaffold.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.6.text",
          "label": "Rowboats",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 10

          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/better_fuel",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/better_fuel.json",
          "text": "Better Fuel",
          "key": "guide-page:name",
          "label": "Better Fuel",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/better_fuel",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/better_fuel.json",
          "text": "Cryo Freezer",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.title",
          "label": "Better Fuel",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/better_fuel",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/better_fuel.json",
          "text": "Fuel is hard to get; it requires venturing out to the ocean, looking for oil spouts. The Cryo Freezer introduces a new way to obtain a better fuel - Cryo Fuel. Cryo Fuel is much more efficient than refined fuel, allowing you to launch a rocket with just one bucket.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Better Fuel",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/better_fuel",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/better_fuel.json",
          "text": "It's created by collecting ice shards, which are found as an ore underground on cold planets. The ice shard then needs to be inserted into a Cryo Freezer, and it will convert to 25 mB of Cryo Fuel. Alternatively, you can convert ice, packed ice and blue ice to fuel. Once you've made at least two buckets (one for a launch and the other for a return trip), place the buckets in the rocket fuel slot.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.text",
          "label": "Better Fuel",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/better_fuel",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/better_fuel.json",
          "text": "Cryo Fuel",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.title",
          "label": "Better Fuel",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/better_fuel",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/better_fuel.json",
          "text": "In addition to being an excellent fuel, cryo fuel has some other features. When placed, it will damage anything inside, similar to lava. It also freezes water, allowing for an infinite ice source.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.text",
          "label": "Better Fuel",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/detection",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/detection.json",
          "text": "Oxygen Sensor",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.title",
          "label": "Detection",
          "item_ref_count": 3,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 2
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/detection",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/detection.json",
          "text": "The oxygen sensor is a block that will emit a redstone signal when it detects oxygen. You can shift-right-click it to invert the signal. Despite its name, you can also change its detection type by right-clicking with a wrench. It has the option to detect oxygen, safe temperature, and normal gravity.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Detection",
          "item_ref_count": 3,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 2
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/energizer",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/energizer.json",
          "text": "The energizer is a machine capable of powering items and storing large amounts of energy. To charge an item, power the energizer and then right-click on the energizer with that item. It stores 2 million energy, making it capable of charging machines for a very long time. It also retains its energy when broken.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Energizer",
          "item_ref_count": 3,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 2
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/flags",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/flags.json",
          "text": "Flags are decorative blocks available in 16 colors. By default, when placed, they will display the face of the flag owner. They can be configured to display a custom image from a URL by right-clicking and entering the URL in the GUI. This will display the internet image onto the flag.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",

          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/fluid_pipes",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/fluid_pipes.json",
          "text": "Fluid pipes are similar to cables but with fluid transfer instead of energy transfer. To use them, craft some fluid pipes and a wrench. Then, right-click on one of the pipes with the wrench to cycle through the pipe's modes: 'none,' 'normal,' 'insert,' and 'extract.'",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Fluid Pipes",
          "item_ref_count": 4,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/fluid_pipes",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/fluid_pipes.json",
          "text": "Pipes with 'insert' set will receive fluid from the network, while pipes with 'extract' set will pump fluid into the network.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.text",
          "label": "Fluid Pipes",
          "item_ref_count": 4,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/fluid_pipes",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/fluid_pipes.json",
          "text": "Like cables, fluid pipes also have a duct block for transfering fluids safely in and out of sealed structures.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.text",
          "label": "Fluid Pipes",
          "item_ref_count": 4,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",

        {

          "text": "Industrial Lamps",

        {

          "text": "Industrial lamps are available in all 16 colors, and in small and large sizes. By default, these will all emit normal white light; however, if you have Shimmer, each light will emit its respective color.",

          "text": "The netherite space suit is an upgraded version of the standard space suit, featuring permanent fire resistance, more protection and the ability to survive on Venus and Mercury.         {
          "kind": "guide_page",

          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/sliding_doors.json",

        },
        {

          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/sliding_doors.json",
          "text": "Sliding Doors",

        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/sliding_doors",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/surviving_mars/sliding_doors.json",
          "text": "Sliding doors are large 3x3 doors. They seal oxygen when closed, making them great candidates for airlocks. They can be locked by shift-right-clicking them with a wrench. Locked doors can only be opened with redstone.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Sliding Doors",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/creating_plates",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/creating_plates.json",
          "text": "Creating Plates",
          "key": "guide-page:name",
          "label": "Creating Plates",
          "item_ref_count": 3,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 2
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/creating_plates",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/creating_plates.json",
          "text": "Once you've got energy, you'll want to craft a compressor. This machine can compress ingots and blocks into plates, which are needed to craft many things.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Creating Plates",
          "item_ref_count": 3,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 2
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/fuel",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/fuel.json",
          "text": "Even after you place your rocket, you will not be able to fly just yet. To fly, you will need to fill the rocket with fuel.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Fuel",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/fuel",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/fuel.json",
          "text": "Obtaining Fuel",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.title",
          "label": "Fuel",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/fuel",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/fuel.json",
          "text": "Oil is found in large amounts spouting over the ocean surface. Collect as much oil as you can, as oil is non-renewable, and you need a lot of it for each rocket launch.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.text",
          "label": "Fuel",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/fuel",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/fuel.json",
          "text": "Obtaining Fuel",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.title",
          "label": "Fuel",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/fuel",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/fuel.json",
          "text": "After collecting enough oil (at least six buckets for a launch and return), you will need to refine the oil into fuel. To do this, you will need to craft a fuel refinery. Power it, then place the oil in the left slot, and it will be refined into fuel.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.text",
          "label": "Fuel",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/fuel",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/fuel.json",
          "text": "Fuel Refinery",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.title",
          "label": "Fuel",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/fuel",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/fuel.json",
          "text": "Shift-click on the rocket to open its inventory, then place three buckets of fuel into the input slot. Place the other three buckets into the rocket's inventory for a return trip.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.text",
          "label": "Fuel",
          "item_ref_count": 6,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/generating_energy",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/generating_energy.json",
          "text": "Generating Energy",
          "key": "guide-page:name",

          "count": 2
        },
        {

          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/generating_energy.json",

        {

          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/generating_energy.json",
          "text": "Energy is core resource of Ad Astra. It's used in all machines and is required for everything. There are multiple ways to generate energy, the most basic way being the coal generator. It uses burnable resources like wood or coal to generate energy.",

      "id": "pack:tfg/ftbquests_chapters_progression",

      "semantic_evidence": [
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/progression",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/progression.snbt",
          "text": "Early game Circuits are made using hand crafting and Create.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Early game Circuits are made using hand crafting and Create.",
          "item_ref_count": 19
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/progression",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/progression.snbt",
          "text": "Have a good Create setup to make them easier to produce then pick up the Circuit Assembler to make them a lot more efficient.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Early game Circuits are made using hand crafting and Create.",
          "item_ref_count": 19
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/progression",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/progression.snbt",

          "label": "Early game Circuits are made using hand crafting and Create.",
          "item_ref_count": 19
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/progression",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/progression.snbt",
          "text": "Craftable in Steam Age and LV",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Early game Circuits are made using hand crafting and Create.",
          "item_ref_count": 19
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/progression",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/progression.snbt",
          "text": "The Integrated Circuit line may not be a strict upgrade in terms of cost, but will tide you over as a stepping stone towards the next tier.",

          "label": "Early game Circuits are made using hand crafting and Create.",
          "item_ref_count": 19
        },
        {

          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/progression.snbt",

          "label": "Early game Circuits are made using hand crafting and Create.",
          "item_ref_count": 19
        },
        {

        },

          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/progression.snbt",

          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/progression.snbt",

          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/progression.snbt",

        },

          "label": "Early game Circuits are made using hand crafting and Create.",
          "item_ref_count": 19
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/progression",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/progression.snbt",
          "text": "This is the Wetware Circuit line.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Early game Circuits are made using hand crafting and Create.",
          "item_ref_count": 19
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/progression",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/progression.snbt",
          "text": "It will be the biggest automation challenge to face in this modpack. Best of luck.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Early game Circuits are made using hand crafting and Create.",
          "item_ref_count": 19
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/progression",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/progression.snbt",
          "text": "Soft and Wetware",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Early game Circuits are made using hand crafting and Create.",
          "item_ref_count": 19
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/progression",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/progression.snbt",
          "text": "Craftable in ZPM",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Early game Circuits are made using hand crafting and Create.",
          "item_ref_count": 19
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/progression",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/progression.snbt",
          "text": "On this line you can see the Steam, or ULV, equivalents.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Early game Circuits are made using hand crafting and Create.",
          "item_ref_count": 19
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/progression",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/progression.snbt",
          "text": "ULV Circuits",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Early game Circuits are made using hand crafting and Create.",
          "item_ref_count": 19
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/progression",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/progression.snbt",
          "text": "Circuits along this line are LV, and are interchangeable in recipes.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Early game Circuits are made using hand crafting and Create.",
          "item_ref_count": 19
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/progression",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/progression.snbt",
          "text": "Electronic Circuits are the worst in terms of cost and effort required to produce, but you have to start somewhere.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Early game Circuits are made using hand crafting and Create.",
          "item_ref_count": 19
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/progression",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/progression.snbt",
          "text": "Basic Integrated Processors act as a middle layer between Electronic Circuits and Microchip Processors.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Early game Circuits are made using hand crafting and Create.",
          "item_ref_count": 19
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/progression",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/progression.snbt",
          "text": "Finally, Microchip Processors will be the best option for the rest of the playthrough.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Early game Circuits are made using hand crafting and Create.",
          "item_ref_count": 19
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/progression",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/progression.snbt",
          "text": "LV Circuits",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Early game Circuits are made using hand crafting and Create.",
          "item_ref_count": 19
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/progression",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/progression.snbt",
          "text": "Circuits along this line are MV, and are interchangeable in recipes.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Early game Circuits are made using hand crafting and Create.",
          "item_ref_count": 19
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/progression",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/progression.snbt",
          "text": "The pattern stays other same: the rightmost circuit comes in larger quantities and is overall easier to produce.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Early game Circuits are made using hand crafting and Create.",
          "item_ref_count": 19
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/progression",

          "item_ref_count": 19
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/progression",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/progression.snbt",
          "text": "Circuits along this line are HV, and are interchangeable in recipes.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Early game Circuits are made using hand crafting and Create.",

        },
        {

          "key": "quest-snbt",

        },
        {

          "item_ref_count": 19
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/progression",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/progression.snbt",
          "text": "Circuits along this line are EV, and are interchangeable in recipes.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Early game Circuits are made using hand crafting and Create.",
          "item_ref_count": 19
        },
        {

        {

          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Early game Circuits are made using hand crafting and Create.",
          "item_ref_count": 19
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/progression",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/progression.snbt",
          "text": "IV Circuits",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Early game Circuits are made using hand crafting and Create.",
          "item_ref_count": 19
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/progression",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/progression.snbt",
          "text": "Circuits along this line are LuV, and are interchangeable in recipes.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Early game Circuits are made using hand crafting and Create.",
          "item_ref_count": 19
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/progression",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/progression.snbt",
          "text": "Make sure to set up wetware board production as soon as possible! These take a very long time to make.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Early game Circuits are made using hand crafting and Create.",
          "item_ref_count": 19
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/progression",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/progression.snbt",
          "text": "It's LuV, not LUV.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Early game Circuits are made using hand crafting and Create.",
          "item_ref_count": 19
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/progression",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/progression.snbt",
          "text": "LuV Circuits",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Early game Circuits are made using hand crafting and Create.",
          "item_ref_count": 19
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/progression",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/progression.snbt",
          "text": "Circuits along this line are ZPM, and are interchangeable in recipes.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Early game Circuits are made using hand crafting and Create.",
          "item_ref_count": 19
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/progression",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/progression.snbt",
          "text": "ZPM Circuits",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Early game Circuits are made using hand crafting and Create.",
          "item_ref_count": 19
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/progression",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/progression.snbt",
          "text": "Circuits along this line are UV, and are interchangeable for each other.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Early game Circuits are made using hand crafting and Create.",
          "item_ref_count": 19
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/progression",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/progression.snbt",
          "text": "UV Circuits",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Early game Circuits are made using hand crafting and Create.",
          "item_ref_count": 19
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/progression",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/progression.snbt",
          "text": "Congratulations! You've reached the end of the circuit progression.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Early game Circuits are made using hand crafting and Create.",
          "item_ref_count": 19
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/progression",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/progression.snbt",
          "text": "The only UHV circuit is the Wetware Mainframe, which is used for crafting some endgame stuff.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Early game Circuits are made using hand crafting and Create.",
          "item_ref_count": 19
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/progression",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/progression.snbt",
          "text": "UHV Circuits",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Early game Circuits are made using hand crafting and Create.",
          "item_ref_count": 19
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/progression",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/progression.snbt",
          "text": "The Circuit Progression of GregTech can seem intimidating at first, but it's not that bad.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Early game Circuits are made using hand crafting and Create.",
          "item_ref_count": 19
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/progression",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/progression.snbt",
          "text": "First, you need to understand that circuits of the same tier are interchangeable. For example, to craft an HV Mixer, you can use any HV-tier circuit.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Early game Circuits are made using hand crafting and Create.",
          "item_ref_count": 19
        }
      ],
