    {
      "id": "pack:tfg/create_logistics",
      "label": "create_logistics",
      "origin": "pack_generated",
      "confidence": 0.65,
      "support": 16,
      "evidence": [
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/create_logistics",
          "confidence": 0.65
        }
      ],
      "semantic_evidence": [
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/create_logistics",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/create_logistics.snbt",
          "text": "create_logistics",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "create_logistics",
          "item_ref_count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/create_logistics",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/create_logistics.snbt",
          "text": "By utilising a few machines from Create, you'll be able to improve your item transportation and storage capabilities significantly. Later on once you progress into the Voltaic Age, you'll even be able to set up processes to automatically order specific items or route them around your base as needed!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "create_logistics",
          "item_ref_count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/create_logistics",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/create_logistics.snbt",
          "text": "As this questline deals primarily with machines from Create, this means Pondering the machines will be your best source of information aside from these quests, so be sure to look through their entries.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "create_logistics",
          "item_ref_count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/create_logistics",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/create_logistics.snbt",
          "text": "Boxing Clever",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "create_logistics",
          "item_ref_count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/create_logistics",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/create_logistics.snbt",
          "text": "all_started",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "create_logistics",
          "item_ref_count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/create_logistics",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/create_logistics.snbt",
          "text": "When provided with a redstone signal, the Packager will wrap up items from the block it is attached to (you can switch the connection side with a wrench) and output a neat little cardboard package. Each cardboard package has 9 slots' worth of inventory, so it's great for moving a bunch of items around at once. To craft the Packager, you need plenty of Wrought Iron and an Electron Tube, not to mention a bunch of Cardboard (Paper and Glue).",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "create_logistics",
          "item_ref_count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/create_logistics",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/create_logistics.snbt",
          "text": "Note that once crafted, the packager works without any additional cardboard, so don't worry about upkeep!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "create_logistics",
          "item_ref_count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/create_logistics",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/create_logistics.snbt",
          "text": "{@pagebreak}",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "create_logistics",
          "item_ref_count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/create_logistics",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/create_logistics.snbt",
          "text": "Putting a Sign on the Packager will make it automatically label the sign's contents onto all packages as a destination address. This will be more useful once you start working with various ways to route packages around.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "create_logistics",
          "item_ref_count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/create_logistics",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/create_logistics.snbt",
          "text": "Packagers can also unwrap any packages that get put into it, unloading their contents into the attached inventory.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "create_logistics",
          "item_ref_count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/create_logistics",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/create_logistics.snbt",
          "text": "Note that Framed Signs do not work for this purpose!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "create_logistics",
          "item_ref_count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/create_logistics",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/create_logistics.snbt",
          "text": "Pack it up",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "create_logistics",
          "item_ref_count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/create_logistics",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/create_logistics.snbt",
          "text": "Packages can be routed using the specialized Package Filter, which can be used just like any filter in any Create machine that supports filter slots. Just right click while holding the filter and type in the address that should be filtered, and any packages with that specified destination address will be let through. This is useful especially for Brass Tunnels to make your belt network route your packages around based on destination.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "create_logistics",
          "item_ref_count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/create_logistics",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/create_logistics.snbt",
          "text": "The general solution to package routing",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "create_logistics",
          "item_ref_count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/create_logistics",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/create_logistics.snbt",
          "text": "Package Filters",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "create_logistics",
          "item_ref_count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/create_logistics",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/create_logistics.snbt",
          "text": "Package Frogports are one of three ways you can move packages around and get them to where you want them to be. They can hang packages onto Chain Conveyor lines be transported along the chain connections. To use them, you first need a network of Chain Conveyors connected to each other using chains and then powered: Any chain with the tag #forge:chains will do. Right click on a chain connection before placing down a Frogport to have it interact with that segment of chain.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "create_logistics",
          "item_ref_count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/create_logistics",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/create_logistics.snbt",
          "text": "Frogports placed directly on top of a Packager automatically hang up packages that are created in said Packager.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "create_logistics",
          "item_ref_count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/create_logistics",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/create_logistics.snbt",
          "text": "Frogports can also receive packages in addition to sending them. If you right click on a Frogport, you can see that it has a name you can edit. This name acts as an address - if any packages that are labelled with a destination address matches its name, the Frogport will automatically collect them by taking them off the chain network! This collected package will be put into the inventory of the block the Frogport is placed on.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "create_logistics",
          "item_ref_count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/create_logistics",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/create_logistics.snbt",
          "text": "As long as the chain network is connected to the input and output Frogports somehow, the package will eventually find its way, although you may want to optimize the paths to reduce package travel time on your own.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "create_logistics",
          "item_ref_count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/create_logistics",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/create_logistics.snbt",
          "text": "The chain of command",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "create_logistics",
          "item_ref_count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/create_logistics",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/create_logistics.snbt",
          "text": "Package Frogports",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "create_logistics",
          "item_ref_count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/create_logistics",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/create_logistics.snbt",
          "text": "Postboxes are used to route packages around using Train Networks. You first right click a Train Station to link the Postbox to that station before placing it, and from then on any trains that stop there will be able to interact with the Postbox (given it is within 4 blocks of the station).",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "create_logistics",
          "item_ref_count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/create_logistics",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/create_logistics.snbt",
          "text": "Much like Package Frogports, they can be named. Passing trains will then pick up any packages whose destination does not match the Postbox's name. Furthermore, if the train has any packages in its inventories that do match the Postbox, they will be passed into the Postbox's inventory instead.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "create_logistics",
          "item_ref_count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/create_logistics",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/create_logistics.snbt",
          "text": "One last cool thing about Postboxes is that when extracting automatically from them (via chutes, funnels, etc), only packages with a destination that matches the Postbox's name will be taken out and any packages with a different destination will remain inside to be loaded onto trains later.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "create_logistics",
          "item_ref_count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/create_logistics",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/create_logistics.snbt",
          "text": "You've got mail",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "create_logistics",
          "item_ref_count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/create_logistics",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/create_logistics.snbt",
          "text": "Here are some other important things that may be useful when trying to route packages around:",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "create_logistics",
          "item_ref_count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/create_logistics",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/create_logistics.snbt",
          "text": "- Packages are randomly created from a selection of four possible items, so stuff like GregTech's Item Filters will be very tedious to set up! We recommend using the Package Filter wherever you can, but...",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "create_logistics",
          "item_ref_count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/create_logistics",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/create_logistics.snbt",
          "text": "- If you really want to try using other methods to move packages around, the destination address is stored as NBT data in the tag Address. For example, a package with the destination address \"Home\" will have in its NBT data: {Address:\"Home\"}.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "create_logistics",
          "item_ref_count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/create_logistics",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/create_logistics.snbt",
          "text": "- Package address filtering actually supports a simplified form of RegEx. This means you can:",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "create_logistics",
          "item_ref_count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/create_logistics",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/create_logistics.snbt",
          "text": "- Match any length string with *",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "create_logistics",
          "item_ref_count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/create_logistics",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/create_logistics.snbt",
          "text": "- Match any single character with ?",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "create_logistics",
          "item_ref_count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/create_logistics",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/create_logistics.snbt",
          "text": "- Match a single character from a group of characters surrounded by [] (e.g. A[cr]t will match 'Act' and 'Art', but not 'Ant')",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "create_logistics",
          "item_ref_count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/create_logistics",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/create_logistics.snbt",
          "text": "- Match a single character that does not belong to a group of characters surrounded by [! ] (e.g. A[!cr]t will match 'Aft' and 'Apt', but not 'Act' or 'Art')",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "create_logistics",
          "item_ref_count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/create_logistics",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/create_logistics.snbt",
          "text": "- Match a string from a list of strings given inside {}(e.g. TerraFirma{Craft,Greg} will match both TerraFirmaCraft and TerraFirmaGreg)",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "create_logistics",
          "item_ref_count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/create_logistics",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/create_logistics.snbt",
          "text": "- All previous symbols can be escaped; For example, A\\* will be read as A* and only match 'A*'.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "create_logistics",
          "item_ref_count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/create_logistics",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/create_logistics.snbt",
          "text": "Other information on packages and addresses",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "create_logistics",
          "item_ref_count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/create_logistics",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/create_logistics.snbt",
          "text": "Other Information",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "create_logistics",
          "item_ref_count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/create_logistics",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/create_logistics.snbt",
          "text": "Tell me more",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "create_logistics",
          "item_ref_count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/create_logistics",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/create_logistics.snbt",
          "text": "one_completed",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "create_logistics",
          "item_ref_count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/create_logistics",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/create_logistics.snbt",
          "text": "Whichever way you obtained the materials needed, you need a Lathe or Belt Grinder to actually create the Lens. Either a Basic Lathe or Belt Grinder will be enough to create the lens from an Exquisite Rose Quartz gem, but if you want to make the lens from a mere plate you will need better equipment in the form of an Advanced Lathe.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "create_logistics",
          "item_ref_count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/create_logistics",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/create_logistics.snbt",
          "text": "Once you've crafted it, the lens can be used in a Basic Laser Engraver with a Double Red Alloy Plate to create the Transmitter unit you need, and won't be consumed in the process.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "create_logistics",
          "item_ref_count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/create_logistics",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/create_logistics.snbt",
          "text": "Note: Once you make it to MV, you'll be able to make more Rose Quartz at home - just be sure to reserve at least one Rose Quartz Gem for it!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "create_logistics",
          "item_ref_count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/create_logistics",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/create_logistics.snbt",
          "text": "YONO (You Only Need One)",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "create_logistics",
          "item_ref_count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/create_logistics",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/create_logistics.snbt",
          "text": "Rose-tinted Glasses",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "create_logistics",
          "item_ref_count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/create_logistics",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/create_logistics.snbt",
          "text": "Moving packages around is good and all, but surely that can't be all there is? Well, you're exactly right. There's much more that can be done with packages, including reading inventory contents and having things be delivered remotely, and later on even automatically sending ingredients to be processed based on an item's threshold!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "create_logistics",
          "item_ref_count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/create_logistics",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/create_logistics.snbt",
          "text": "But before delving into High Logistics, some preparations are in order.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "create_logistics",
          "item_ref_count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/create_logistics",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/create_logistics.snbt",
          "text": "The Transmitters that would be required for crafting such equipment can only realistically be produced via high-energy light refracted through a very unique mineral: Rose Quartz. In other words, we need both a Basic Laser Engraver and some way of obtaining a Rose Quartz Lens, so you better get to getting these done, eh?",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "create_logistics",
          "item_ref_count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/create_logistics",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/create_logistics.snbt",
          "text": "...But Rose Quartz is Pink",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "create_logistics",
          "item_ref_count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/create_logistics",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/create_logistics.snbt",
          "text": "Roses are red...",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "create_logistics",
          "item_ref_count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/create_logistics",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/create_logistics.snbt",
          "text": "one_started",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "create_logistics",
          "item_ref_count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/create_logistics",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/create_logistics.snbt",
          "text": "The only natural source of Rose Quartz of a high enough quality to easily craft the Lens out of is located far, far to the north in freezing temperatures (below -10°C). Getting Kaolin clay flashbacks yet?",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "create_logistics",
          "item_ref_count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/create_logistics",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/create_logistics.snbt",
          "text": "Rose Quartz only forms in the cores of ancient glaciers. Although not common, the glacier core is easily identified by their Blue Ice outer shells. The Rose Quartz will be found inside - first as ore, then as compressed raw ore blocks in the very center.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "create_logistics",
          "item_ref_count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/create_logistics",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/create_logistics.snbt",
          "text": "What we need is Exquisite Rose Quartz, which you can get by crushing, washing, and then sifting the raw ore you mined; The Exquisite gem has a 3 percent chance of being sifted out, but you only need one such gem to proceed.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "create_logistics",
          "item_ref_count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/create_logistics",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/create_logistics.snbt",
          "text": "Do not smelt the ore as this will only get you normal quality Rose Quartz!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "create_logistics",
          "item_ref_count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/create_logistics",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/create_logistics.snbt",
          "text": "The same preparations you once may have made for Kaolin clay will apply here as well: Prepare a lot of food, a source of portable water to drink, and some transportation (boats are nice, but aircraft should be well within your reach at this point).",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "create_logistics",
          "item_ref_count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/create_logistics",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/create_logistics.snbt",
          "text": "If you created your world prior to TFG 0.12 or are using the older worldgen, rose quartz can be found in low elevation biomes such as the Ocean, Shores, and Lowlands. Otherwise, if you're using the new worldgen, you'll want to look for Ice Sheets and Glaciers.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "create_logistics",
          "item_ref_count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/create_logistics",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/create_logistics.snbt",
          "text": "The North Remembers",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "create_logistics",
          "item_ref_count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/create_logistics",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/create_logistics.snbt",
          "text": "Finding Rose Quartz",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "create_logistics",
          "item_ref_count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/create_logistics",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/create_logistics.snbt",
          "text": "The first High Logistics machine you can make is a Stock Link, which can be used to create a Logistics Network. When placed onto any side of a Packager, the Stock Link will make the inventory the Packager is attached to visible to the network! On its own, the Stock Link isn't super useful, but it is a core component of all the other machines yet to come...",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "create_logistics",
          "item_ref_count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/create_logistics",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/create_logistics.snbt",
          "text": "Remember to right click on an existing Stock Link before placing one down to tune the two to share the same network. To cancel this, you can simply craft the Stock Link by itself to clear its attunement.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "create_logistics",
          "item_ref_count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/create_logistics",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/create_logistics.snbt",
          "text": "We got storage buses at home",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "create_logistics",
          "item_ref_count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/create_logistics",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/create_logistics.snbt",
          "text": "Stock Link",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "create_logistics",
          "item_ref_count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/create_logistics",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/create_logistics.snbt",
          "text": "If you've already used Stock Links to provide various items to a Logistics Network, the next step is to make something that can call in said items remotely... And that's where the Stock Ticker comes in. Much like the Stock Link, you first need to right click an existing link to attune the Ticker to that network. You then need to place down a Blaze Burner or any mob in a Seat next to the Stock Ticker to have them work as a Stock Keeper.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "create_logistics",
          "item_ref_count": 16
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/create_logistics",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/create_logistics.snbt",
          "text": "Now that the poor creature is indentured for life, you can click on them to see all the items on the Logistics Network. You can even search for items in the network, and create an order to be sent to specific addresses! There's no range limitation for this, although you do need to create your own way of routing around the packages that will be ordered through the Stock Links as well as have the link in question chunkloaded for this to work.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "create_logistics",
          "item_ref_count": 16
        }
      ],
      "aliases": [],
      "description": "Candidate player storage section inferred from pack evidence; accept only if it is a useful way to group items."
    },

        {

          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/queststfg",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/queststfg.snbt",
          "text": "Thank you for starting TerraFirmaGreg Modern!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "https://github.com/TerraFirmaGreg-Team/Modpack-Modern/wiki/%5BEN%5D-Suggested-additional-mods",
          "item_ref_count": 15
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/queststfg",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/queststfg.snbt",
          "text": "In this modpack, you will be put to the task of taking control of untouched wilderness and turning it to an industrial wonderland. Start from a humble beginning picking up rocks, and end while traversing interstellar space!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "https://github.com/TerraFirmaGreg-Team/Modpack-Modern/wiki/%5BEN%5D-Suggested-additional-mods",
          "item_ref_count": 15
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/queststfg",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/queststfg.snbt",
          "text": "Survival Modpack As It Should've Been",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "https://github.com/TerraFirmaGreg-Team/Modpack-Modern/wiki/%5BEN%5D-Suggested-additional-mods",
          "item_ref_count": 15
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/queststfg",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/queststfg.snbt",
          "text": "Welcome to TerraFirmaGreg!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "https://github.com/TerraFirmaGreg-Team/Modpack-Modern/wiki/%5BEN%5D-Suggested-additional-mods",
          "item_ref_count": 15
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/queststfg",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/queststfg.snbt",
          "text": "You can create a team to complete quests together. To do this, open your inventory, then in the upper left corner select the button where 3 colorful people are drawn, then an interface will open in which you can create a team. Click the Create a team button, then give it a name and possibly some other parameters, and after successfully creating a team, you can invite other players to it using the plus button in the green circle in the upper right corner. From this point onwards, your quests will be synchronized and any team member will be able to complete them. Good luck!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "https://github.com/TerraFirmaGreg-Team/Modpack-Modern/wiki/%5BEN%5D-Suggested-additional-mods",
          "item_ref_count": 15
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/queststfg",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/queststfg.snbt",
          "text": "Do you play with friends?",

          "label": "https://github.com/TerraFirmaGreg-Team/Modpack-Modern/wiki/%5BEN%5D-Suggested-additional-mods",
          "item_ref_count": 15
        },
        {

          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/queststfg.snbt",

          "label": "https://github.com/TerraFirmaGreg-Team/Modpack-Modern/wiki/%5BEN%5D-Suggested-additional-mods",
          "item_ref_count": 15
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/queststfg",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/queststfg.snbt",
          "text": "How to create a team?",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "https://github.com/TerraFirmaGreg-Team/Modpack-Modern/wiki/%5BEN%5D-Suggested-additional-mods",
          "item_ref_count": 15
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/queststfg",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/queststfg.snbt",
          "text": "If you play on a server, you might want to claim your territory so other players can't interfere. Use the Open Claims key (default \"Ctrl+M\") to open the window, then left-click to claim a chunk and right-click to unclaim them.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "https://github.com/TerraFirmaGreg-Team/Modpack-Modern/wiki/%5BEN%5D-Suggested-additional-mods",
          "item_ref_count": 15
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/queststfg",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/queststfg.snbt",
          "text": "There is a limit to this, so you can't claim everything. You can shift-left-click on a chunk to forceload (aka \"chunk load\") it, and shift-right-click to un-forceload it. Claiming chunks will include your whole team, if you're using one.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "https://github.com/TerraFirmaGreg-Team/Modpack-Modern/wiki/%5BEN%5D-Suggested-additional-mods",
          "item_ref_count": 15
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/queststfg",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/queststfg.snbt",
          "text": "And how to load chunks in your territory",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "https://github.com/TerraFirmaGreg-Team/Modpack-Modern/wiki/%5BEN%5D-Suggested-additional-mods",
          "item_ref_count": 15
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/queststfg",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/queststfg.snbt",
          "text": "How to capture territory?",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "https://github.com/TerraFirmaGreg-Team/Modpack-Modern/wiki/%5BEN%5D-Suggested-additional-mods",
          "item_ref_count": 15
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/queststfg",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/queststfg.snbt",
          "text": "The quests are still in development, and we're always working on improving them! A lot of information about specific mechanics are also in your Field Guide, accessible via the Book tab in your inventory.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "https://github.com/TerraFirmaGreg-Team/Modpack-Modern/wiki/%5BEN%5D-Suggested-additional-mods",
          "item_ref_count": 15
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/queststfg",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/queststfg.snbt",
          "text": "Remember that both JEI and the Field Guide are your friends, as not everything will be explained through quests.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "https://github.com/TerraFirmaGreg-Team/Modpack-Modern/wiki/%5BEN%5D-Suggested-additional-mods",
          "item_ref_count": 15
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/queststfg",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/queststfg.snbt",
          "text": "no_toast",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "https://github.com/TerraFirmaGreg-Team/Modpack-Modern/wiki/%5BEN%5D-Suggested-additional-mods",
          "item_ref_count": 15
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/queststfg",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/queststfg.snbt",
          "text": "I need to read more?",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "https://github.com/TerraFirmaGreg-Team/Modpack-Modern/wiki/%5BEN%5D-Suggested-additional-mods",
          "item_ref_count": 15
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/queststfg",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/queststfg.snbt",
          "text": "Important information about quests!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "https://github.com/TerraFirmaGreg-Team/Modpack-Modern/wiki/%5BEN%5D-Suggested-additional-mods",
          "item_ref_count": 15
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/queststfg",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/queststfg.snbt",
          "text": "Here's a repeatable quest if you want physical copies of the quest book and field guide again.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "https://github.com/TerraFirmaGreg-Team/Modpack-Modern/wiki/%5BEN%5D-Suggested-additional-mods",
          "item_ref_count": 15
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/queststfg",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/queststfg.snbt",
          "text": "Need the books again?",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "https://github.com/TerraFirmaGreg-Team/Modpack-Modern/wiki/%5BEN%5D-Suggested-additional-mods",
          "item_ref_count": 15
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/queststfg",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/queststfg.snbt",
          "text": "If you're playing on a server, by default you only have a small amount of forceload chunks available.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "https://github.com/TerraFirmaGreg-Team/Modpack-Modern/wiki/%5BEN%5D-Suggested-additional-mods",
          "item_ref_count": 15
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/queststfg",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/queststfg.snbt",
          "text": "Server owners can customize this (check the README.txt file!), but if you've got operator permissions (or have cheats enabled), you can also use /ftbchunks admin extra_force_load_chunks @p set X, where X is the new limit.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "https://github.com/TerraFirmaGreg-Team/Modpack-Modern/wiki/%5BEN%5D-Suggested-additional-mods",
          "item_ref_count": 15
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/queststfg",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/queststfg.snbt",
          "text": "How to increase my chunkload limit?",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "https://github.com/TerraFirmaGreg-Team/Modpack-Modern/wiki/%5BEN%5D-Suggested-additional-mods",
          "item_ref_count": 15
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/queststfg",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/queststfg.snbt",
          "text": "If you want to add more content to your modpack, you may be interested in our list of optional mods!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "https://github.com/TerraFirmaGreg-Team/Modpack-Modern/wiki/%5BEN%5D-Suggested-additional-mods",
          "item_ref_count": 15
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/queststfg",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/queststfg.snbt",
          "text": "These are either mods that only work for certain PC setups, would add too much bloat if they were added for everyone, or are too niche to include by default.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "https://github.com/TerraFirmaGreg-Team/Modpack-Modern/wiki/%5BEN%5D-Suggested-additional-mods",
          "item_ref_count": 15
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/queststfg",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/queststfg.snbt",
          "text": "Please note that these mods or their compatibility is not ensured by the TFG dev team, so they may stop functioning at any point in the future.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "https://github.com/TerraFirmaGreg-Team/Modpack-Modern/wiki/%5BEN%5D-Suggested-additional-mods",
          "item_ref_count": 15
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/queststfg",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/queststfg.snbt",
          "text": "Click the globe image next to the quest to open the mod list.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "https://github.com/TerraFirmaGreg-Team/Modpack-Modern/wiki/%5BEN%5D-Suggested-additional-mods",
          "item_ref_count": 15
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/queststfg",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/queststfg.snbt",
          "text": "Customize your experience",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "https://github.com/TerraFirmaGreg-Team/Modpack-Modern/wiki/%5BEN%5D-Suggested-additional-mods",
          "item_ref_count": 15
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/queststfg",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/queststfg.snbt",
          "text": "Optional Mods",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "https://github.com/TerraFirmaGreg-Team/Modpack-Modern/wiki/%5BEN%5D-Suggested-additional-mods",
          "item_ref_count": 15
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/queststfg",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/queststfg.snbt",
          "text": "TerraFirmaGreg - Modern",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "https://github.com/TerraFirmaGreg-Team/Modpack-Modern/wiki/%5BEN%5D-Suggested-additional-mods",
          "item_ref_count": 15
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/queststfg",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/queststfg.snbt",
          "text": "Thank you for starting TerraFirmaGreg Modern!",
          "key": "quest-snbt-lang:quests.tfg.welcome.desc",
          "label": "https://github.com/TerraFirmaGreg-Team/Modpack-Modern/wiki/%5BEN%5D-Suggested-additional-mods",
          "item_ref_count": 15
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/queststfg",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/queststfg.snbt",
          "text": "In this modpack, you will be put to the task of taking control of untouched wilderness and turning it to an industrial wonderland. Start from a humble beginning picking up rocks, and end while traversing interstellar space!",
          "key": "quest-snbt-lang:quests.tfg.welcome.desc",
          "label": "https://github.com/TerraFirmaGreg-Team/Modpack-Modern/wiki/%5BEN%5D-Suggested-additional-mods",
          "item_ref_count": 15
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/queststfg",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/queststfg.snbt",
          "text": "Survival Modpack As It Should've Been",
          "key": "quest-snbt-lang:quests.tfg.welcome.subtitle",
          "label": "https://github.com/TerraFirmaGreg-Team/Modpack-Modern/wiki/%5BEN%5D-Suggested-additional-mods",
          "item_ref_count": 15
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/queststfg",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/queststfg.snbt",
          "text": "Welcome to TerraFirmaGreg!",
          "key": "quest-snbt-lang:quests.tfg.welcome.title",
          "label": "https://github.com/TerraFirmaGreg-Team/Modpack-Modern/wiki/%5BEN%5D-Suggested-additional-mods",
          "item_ref_count": 15
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/queststfg",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/queststfg.snbt",
          "text": "You can create a team to complete quests together. To do this, open your inventory, then in the upper left corner select the button where 3 colorful people are drawn, then an interface will open in which you can create a team. Click the Create a team button, then give it a name and possibly some other parameters, and after successfully creating a team, you can invite other players to it using the plus button in the green circle in the upper right corner. From this point onwards, your quests will be synchronized and any team member will be able to complete them. Good luck!",
          "key": "quest-snbt-lang:quests.tfg.create_team.desc",
          "label": "https://github.com/TerraFirmaGreg-Team/Modpack-Modern/wiki/%5BEN%5D-Suggested-additional-mods",
          "item_ref_count": 15
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/queststfg",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/queststfg.snbt",
          "text": "Do you play with friends?",
          "key": "quest-snbt-lang:quests.tfg.create_team.subtitle",
          "label": "https://github.com/TerraFirmaGreg-Team/Modpack-Modern/wiki/%5BEN%5D-Suggested-additional-mods",
          "item_ref_count": 15
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/queststfg",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/queststfg.snbt",
          "text": "Click here to complete this quest/task",
          "key": "quest-snbt-lang:quests.tasktype.checkmark",
          "label": "https://github.com/TerraFirmaGreg-Team/Modpack-Modern/wiki/%5BEN%5D-Suggested-additional-mods",
          "item_ref_count": 15
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/queststfg",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/queststfg.snbt",
          "text": "How to create a team?",
          "key": "quest-snbt-lang:quests.tfg.create_team.title",
          "label": "https://github.com/TerraFirmaGreg-Team/Modpack-Modern/wiki/%5BEN%5D-Suggested-additional-mods",
          "item_ref_count": 15
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/queststfg",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/queststfg.snbt",
          "text": "If you play on a server, you might want to claim your territory so other players can't interfere. Use the Open Claims key (default \"Ctrl+M\") to open the window, then left-click to claim a chunk and right-click to unclaim them.",
          "key": "quest-snbt-lang:quests.tfg.capture_territory.desc",
          "label": "https://github.com/TerraFirmaGreg-Team/Modpack-Modern/wiki/%5BEN%5D-Suggested-additional-mods",
          "item_ref_count": 15
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/queststfg",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/queststfg.snbt",
          "text": "There is a limit to this, so you can't claim everything. You can shift-left-click on a chunk to forceload (aka \"chunk load\") it, and shift-right-click to un-forceload it. Claiming chunks will include your whole team, if you're using one.",
          "key": "quest-snbt-lang:quests.tfg.capture_territory.desc",
          "label": "https://github.com/TerraFirmaGreg-Team/Modpack-Modern/wiki/%5BEN%5D-Suggested-additional-mods",
          "item_ref_count": 15
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/queststfg",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/queststfg.snbt",
          "text": "And how to load chunks in your territory",
          "key": "quest-snbt-lang:quests.tfg.capture_territory.subtitle",
          "label": "https://github.com/TerraFirmaGreg-Team/Modpack-Modern/wiki/%5BEN%5D-Suggested-additional-mods",
          "item_ref_count": 15
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/queststfg",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/queststfg.snbt",
          "text": "How to capture territory?",
          "key": "quest-snbt-lang:quests.tfg.capture_territory.title",
          "label": "https://github.com/TerraFirmaGreg-Team/Modpack-Modern/wiki/%5BEN%5D-Suggested-additional-mods",
          "item_ref_count": 15
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/queststfg",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/queststfg.snbt",
          "text": "The quests are still in development, and we're always working on improving them! A lot of information about specific mechanics are also in your Field Guide, accessible via the Book tab in your inventory.",
          "key": "quest-snbt-lang:quests.tfg.field_guide.desc",

        },
        {

          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/queststfg.snbt",
          "text": "Remember that both JEI and the Field Guide are your friends, as not everything will be explained through quests.",
          "key": "quest-snbt-lang:quests.tfg.field_guide.desc",
          "label": "https://github.com/TerraFirmaGreg-Team/Modpack-Modern/wiki/%5BEN%5D-Suggested-additional-mods",

          "text": "I need to read more?",
          "key": "quest-snbt-lang:quests.tfg.field_guide.subtitle",
          "label": "https://github.com/TerraFirmaGreg-Team/Modpack-Modern/wiki/%5BEN%5D-Suggested-additional-mods",
          "item_ref_count": 15
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/queststfg",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/queststfg.snbt",

          "label": "https://github.com/TerraFirmaGreg-Team/Modpack-Modern/wiki/%5BEN%5D-Suggested-additional-mods",
          "item_ref_count": 15
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/queststfg",

          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/queststfg.snbt",
          "text": "If you're playing on a server, by default you only have a small amount of forceload chunks available.",
          "key": "quest-snbt-lang:quests.tfg.chunk_limit.desc",

          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/queststfg.snbt",
          "text": "Server owners can customize this (check the README.txt file!), but if you've got operator permissions (or have cheats enabled), you can also use /ftbchunks admin extra_force_load_chunks @p set X, where X is the new limit.",
          "key": "quest-snbt-lang:quests.tfg.chunk_limit.desc",

          "key": "quest-snbt-lang:quests.tfg.optional_mods.desc",
          "label": "https://github.com/TerraFirmaGreg-Team/Modpack-Modern/wiki/%5BEN%5D-Suggested-additional-mods",

          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/progression.snbt",
          "text": "It will be the biggest automation challenge to face in this modpack. Best of luck.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Early game Circuits are made using hand crafting and Create.",
          "item_ref_count": 19
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",

          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/progression.snbt",
          "text": "Soft and Wetware",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Early game Circuits are made using hand crafting and Create.",
          "item_ref_count": 19
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/progression",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/progression.snbt",

          "label": "Early game Circuits are made using hand crafting and Create.",

        {
          "kind": "quest_node",

          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/progression.snbt",
          "text": "On this line you can see the Steam, or ULV, equivalents.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Early game Circuits are made using hand crafting and Create.",

        },
        {

          "label": "Early game Circuits are made using hand crafting and Create.",
          "item_ref_count": 19
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/progression",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/progression.snbt",
          "text": "Circuits along this line are LV, and are interchangeable in recipes.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Early game Circuits are made using hand crafting and Create.",
          "item_ref_count": 19
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/progression",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/progression.snbt",
          "text": "Electronic Circuits are the worst in terms of cost and effort required to produce, but you have to start somewhere.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Early game Circuits are made using hand crafting and Create.",
          "item_ref_count": 19
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/progression",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/progression.snbt",
          "text": "Basic Integrated Processors act as a middle layer between Electronic Circuits and Microchip Processors.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Early game Circuits are made using hand crafting and Create.",
          "item_ref_count": 19
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/progression",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/progression.snbt",
          "text": "Finally, Microchip Processors will be the best option for the rest of the playthrough.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Early game Circuits are made using hand crafting and Create.",
          "item_ref_count": 19
        },
        {

          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/progression.snbt",

          "label": "Early game Circuits are made using hand crafting and Create.",
          "item_ref_count": 19
        },
        {
